CONNETQUOT RIVER DISTRIBUTARY NEAR OAKDALE NY, USGS site 01306495 (USGS-01306495), stream in Suffolk County, New York, draining 27.66 square miles. Daily means from 1971-10-06 to 2015-09-29, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
